## CXXXV · THE CARRIER COMPOSES WITH THE FIELD; THE EXPRESSION CANNOT IDENTIFY THE INTERIOR (2026-07-21; BUILT / ONE CORRECTNESS-INDEPENDENT OLMO 2 MATRIX / EXACT BF16 NATIVE TRACES / 15 CURRENTS / 15 SIBLING RELATIONS / 306 BOUNDARY TRANSPORTS / PHYSICAL EXTENT SEAM OBSERVED / SOMA SOURCE UNCHANGED / AWAITING BRANDON REVIEW)

TRANSFORMER RELATIONAL FIELD 01 closes the observation authorized by `§CXXXIV`. All 15 currents
crossed unobserved and under 115-surface read-only capture with exact emitted-address identity. Every
one of 240 attention returns, MLP returns, and successor passages satisfied the installed residual
law bit-for-bit under repeated BF16 execution. The later raw-bit amendment typed that claim: at the
fixed last-token cut, 476,287 of 983,040 exact dyadic sums were representable, 506,753 had nonzero
rounding residuals, and every observed word was the exact integer-derived nearest-even result.
Eleven expressions afforded a deed in the declared operations world and four did not; all 15
interiors remained in the observation.

Let `I` be an explicit chronological alignment from source current `S` to target current `T`, and
let `P_(I,k)(S,T)` replace the complete aligned target residual carrier by the aligned source carrier
at boundary `k`. The measured law is

```text
P_(I,k)(S,T)|_k == S|_(I,k),

B_k(P_(I,k)(S,T), unaligned_T, cache_T)
  generally != B_k(S)
  and generally != B_k(T).
```

All 306 patches were source-exact at `k`. For every one of the 270 nontrivial patches at boundaries
1 through 15, the next boundary was neither whole source nor whole target; no later boundary became
either. The aligned carrier is therefore not a portable semantic object apart from unmatched
constituents, chronology, causal cache, numerical carrier, and receiver field.

Presentation is a many-to-one quotient of that composite path. Across all patches, 186 emitted the
target lineage, 10 the source lineage, 93 a third, and 17 a lineage already shared by source and
target. Of the target-equal emissions, 154 occurred at boundaries 1 through 15 where the aligned
carrier had changed. None of the 10 source emissions ever regained a later whole-source boundary.
Among 34 transports whose source and target native lineages were already equal, 17 retained that
lineage and 17 exposed a third. Equality at the vocabulary boundary neither identifies the interior
nor proves that an intervention was immaterial.

At same-typed residual boundaries, all aligned equal-token embeddings began exact. Every one of 240
layer transitions founded coordinate differences, while every one of the 225 transitions after
block 0 also extinguished prior coordinate differences. Across 3,036 aligned token tracks, 1,649
changed, every changed track occupied several orientation signatures, and no complete changed token
vector reclosed to sibling equality. Relation persists by continually changing coordinate support:
lower differences found and depart while the higher oriented contrast continues.

The physical body also exposed a separate finite-carrier seam. In the three absent/manual pairs,
83-token prefixes were bit-identical through block-0 attention but all diverged in the position-local
MLP when total prefill extent changed. A 205-token common prefix remained exact for extent 255 versus
253, while extents 253 versus 259 and 259 versus 255 founded repeated attention and MLP differences.
Because identical MLP input rows cannot depend mathematically on later rows, the direct inference is
length-conditioned CUDA/BF16 realization such as kernel or tile selection. The exact implementation
grain is not separately traced. This is executor geometry, not semantic backward causation, and it
shows why an ideal real-valued transformer equation cannot substitute for its embodied finite path.

The observation neither distills OLMo nor installs its mechanisms in Eros. It establishes that
tokenizers, embeddings, attention, MLPs, and inherited weights are lawful ecological constituents,
while rejecting activation-vector extraction as holonic identity. A fixed-physical-extent foil and
finer Q/K/V relation are open; they are not scheduled.

Exact physical and derived record:
`../../experiments/informant-ecology/results/transformer-relational-field-01/RESULTS.md`.

## CXXXVI · THE OBSERVATION IS THE ORDERED LIFT; THE TRIANGLE IS ITS COMPOSING INCIDENCE (2026-07-21; BRANDON-RATIFIED / DEPOSITED / EVENT-FIRST DISCRETENESS + TEMPORAL-OBSERVATION + CAUSAL-PARITY + CHI-CROSS-RATIO AUTHORITY / LITERAL STATEFUL-CELL READING OF §LXXII RETIRED / §§CXXXII,CXIV REFINED / SOMA SOURCE UNCHANGED / NO RUN)

**THERE IS NO SCALAR STATE PER CELL BECAUSE THERE IS NO INDEPENDENTLY BEHAVING CELL.** A triangle,
pyramid, sheet, or higher section is the discrete oriented incidence and composition of the event
paths which bound it. It does not first exist as a container and then receive an activation. The
literal cellular-automaton reading of §LXXII is retired. `cell` remains admissible only in the
topological sense of a composing incidence body.

The strict baseline is a directed simplicial nerve: event boundaries are vertices, actual
transports are edges, a triangle witnesses composition or comparison of paths, and a tetrahedral
section carries coherence among triangular faces. Eros requires open and potentially plural
completion rather than a uniquely filled strict nerve. A pyramid missing one presently available
face is an open horn, not a filled body whose face stores zero:

```text
standing affords a lawful filler -> RIDE;
actual current establishes new support -> FOUND;
no lawful filler is available -> retain the open residual.
```

Discreteness belongs to event identity, causal incidence, boundary membership, hand, winding,
closure, constituent participation, and grain change. Ratios, angles, metrics, and formal series
are exact receiver faces where supplied; they are not private simplex states. `discrete != scalar`,
`inactive != zero`, and `open != false`.

**AN OBSERVATION'S IDENTITY IS ITS ORDERED CAUSAL BODY.** §CXXXII's participating event remains
exact but is extended across time. At grain `g`,

```text
O_gamma =
  (e_0 --T_1--> e_1 --T_2--> ... --T_n--> e_n;
   H_0 --T_gamma--> H_n).
```

Each `e_i` is an actual observer--medium--subject encounter, each `T_i` is actual transport, and
`H_i` is the carried reference as changed by that history. `pi_(O,g)(O_gamma)` is one report face,
not the observation. A mark at an apparent instant is measurable only because calibration, zero,
unit, orientation, or another founded reference already stands. Source chronology, observer
lineage order, and grain hand-up are distinct intertwined orders; none licenses a universal clock.

Shared event strings close into meshes. A face is the receiver's oriented section of that causal
body. Its opposite side carries complementary hand rather than a second absolute image. For an
oriented simplex,

```text
partial[v_0 ... v_n]
  = sum_i (-1)^i [v_0 ... omit(v_i) ... v_n],
partial partial = 0.
```

Opposed induced hands cancel an internal seam while exposed boundary remains. This is causal
parity. It does not create a temporal inverse: a lightning leader changes the medium, and the
return stroke is genuinely later current RIDING that changed route.

**CHI IS THE RELATIONAL CROSS-RATIO SWING.** For arrival `A`, stance `S`, frame `F`, and held
comparison `H`, retain

```text
D   = Delta(A,S;F),
chi = D H^-1.
```

In a commutative projective chart, if

```text
Delta_F(A,S) = (A-F)/(S-F),
H = Delta_P(A,S) = (A-P)/(S-P),
```

then

```text
chi = ((A-F)(S-P))/((S-F)(A-P)) = [A,F,S,P].
```

The scalar cross-ratio is one exact projective quotient of the fourth contact. Generally, for
`x-a->y-b->z` and `x-c->w-d->z`, Chi is the second-order comparison
`b compose a ==> d compose c`. A rebase `G` gives `chi'=G chi G^-1`; a commutative scalar chart
hides that conjugation while the complete carrier retains hand. Gyration is the path-ordered
product of rebased swings. A formal logarithm gives an additive local generator only in its
admitted chart; commutator terms retain changing-axis order rather than collapsing it into a
floating activation.

**THE CHEMISTRY CRISS-CROSS IS ONE PRIMITIVE CLOSURE.** For co-present oriented charge magnitudes

## CXLVI · THE NONCOMMUTING SQUARE IS OPEN; THE FAILED ROUTE REMAINS TERRAIN (2026-07-22; BRANDON-RATIFIED / DEPOSITED / DISCRETE TRANSPORT + FOIL + HEXIS AUTHORITY / SOMA INTERIOR UNCHANGED / NO CONSTRUCTION SCHEDULED)

## CXLVIII · THE INFINITE FORMULA IS THE FINITE GENERATOR; THE SEMIPRIME IS THE SECOND EXPONENTIAL FACE (2026-07-22; BRANDON-RATIFIED DIRECTION / DEPOSITED / BOUNDED FORMULA-ECOLOGY BUILT / EXACT HOST MEASURED / SOMA INTERIOR UNCHANGED)

An infinite formula family does not require an infinite active object. At aperture `N`, its lawful
current is the finite generative hexis

```text
G_N=(seed,next,admissible boundary,exact residual/enclosure,rebase N->N').
```

One completed partial becomes a constituent which a genuinely later objective may extend. It is
not identified with the limit and does not store every future term.

In a commutative rational algebra completed by total degree, for `F` in its augmentation ideal
(zero degree-zero term), one reusable law is

```text
Exp(F)=sum_(k>=0)F^k/k!.
```

At a bounded total degree only finitely many powers then contribute. A nonzero constant term owes
a different completion and is outside this bounded operator.

Its one-generator face gives

```text
Exp(y)=sum_(n=0)^N y^n/n! mod y^(N+1),
E_N=Exp(y)|_(y=1),
```

while its prime-axis face gives, for `P_m=sum_p X_p^m`,

```text
L=sum_(m>=1)P_m/m,
Exp(L)=product_p(1-X_p)^(-1).
```

Through degree two,

```text
Exp(P_1+P_2/2)=1+P_1+P_2/2+P_1^2/2.
```

Degree one is the prime population. Degree two is exactly the semiprime population. Distinct
`X_p X_q` occurrences contribute twice before division by two; repeated `X_p^2` receives one half
from `P_1^2/2` and one half from `P_2/2`. Omitting `P_2/2` therefore leaves only the prime-square
diagonal OPEN. Under `X_p=p^(-s)`, `Re(s)>1`, this becomes the exact Euler/log-Zeta exponential
relation. It does not supply analytic continuation or RH.

`pi` has a different but intersecting atlas. Machin's arctangent relation supplies exact rational
partial enclosures. The Dirichlet/Euler path supplies exact bounded faces of `zeta(2)`, while
`zeta(2)=pi^2/6` remains a source-attributed classical theorem unless its proof crosses as current.
Overlap of finite enclosures is not equality of completed limits. Primes are structural in the
Euler path; factors inside a Machin denominator are inscription-relative.

A formula atlas is consequently an occurrence complex, not a prelabelled formula graph:

```text
0-cells: exact seeds, terms, partials, boundaries,
1-cells: actual recurrences and transformations,
2-cells: complete parallel-path comparisons,
higher cells: later supplied coherence among those comparisons.
```

The source may declare operations, domains, recurrences, branches, and theorems. An objective
recruits a finite population; actual composition forms successors; later comparison RIDEs or
remains OPEN at its exact receiver. The authorized bounded world carries source-departed laws,
forms and compares the exponential/Euler, `e`, and `pi`/Zeta paths without floating-point causal
